##### It’s about time… Who won the marketing game during the first weeks of 2026? And the rest is advertising, collaborations, and corporate at its worst and best!?

This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. *Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.*

> Brand & Advertising coverage from 05.01 — 17.01.2026….

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!JnHP!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/d8b7db11-42e9-439a-8a99-52dc8afb8a96?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* SXSW Sydney [scrapped](https://substack.com/redirect/bb02e2e7-b576-4497-8931-19ee82d412d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after three years [due to](https://substack.com/redirect/cbaf663f-250a-44f5-9542-fb9763deb90f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ‘changing global environment’, organisers announce.
* Humble [Hershey’s](https://substack.com/redirect/a099acf0-c48d-4558-bacb-aeb0a54d3545?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gets a marketing makeover.
* Influencers and OnlyFans models are increasingly [requesting O-1 visas](https://substack.com/redirect/bda9fb43-83a6-4f12-bd08-2362d710cd97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): ‘This is the American dream now.’

* Luxury department store chain Saks [files for bankruptcy](https://substack.com/redirect/db97f347-da6b-47f3-8e1c-98e65601a9bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Amazon [threatens](https://substack.com/redirect/aa5dd4aa-537f-4787-8986-c10281e64027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ‘drastic’ action after Saks bankruptcy, says $475M stake is now worthless.
* [2016](https://substack.com/redirect/5543e4bd-e9d1-4337-a187-d1bea64bed9d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Was Actually [The Worst Year](https://substack.com/redirect/48d25cf0-1117-401c-bfcd-35ab17017ee3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Ever, [Remember](https://substack.com/redirect/13f9d627-998c-44e8-8b07-0328ba91a96f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)?

---

In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

##### Tracksuit’s [Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) confirms that effective collaborations aren’t about reach, but about resonance.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5vyc!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5df9586f-b9d8-4e69-a660-6453f56f1b5a_1699x624.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/aef9a460-ccc1-4bf2-baea-5c9cdb9aa049?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)The biggest lesson from the year’s top sneaker collaborations is that ***scale is secondary, and specificity is everything.*** The partnerships that created the most impact weren’t the loudest, the most famous, or most widely seen. 

[Their data](https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shows that the collaborations that moved brands forward weren’t designed for everyone, they were designed for someone. That is, they were built for a small, passionate audience, which proved to be more valuable than millions of passive viewers. 

For example, Tracksuit and Bimma’s top collaboration ([download the report](https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to find out which one this is!) achieved only 4% general awareness, but 67% among fans familiar with the brand and collaborator. Alignment is the engine.

“The strongest collaborations don’t chase virality. They build meaning,” said Matt Herbert, Co-founder of [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). “When brands design for the right audience with the right partner, they create depth instead of noise.”

> **The 2025 Collab of the Year report is [a new playbook](https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for marketers:** 

> You can use the [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help guide your own brand collaborations, regardless of category. This is a framework that breaks down the ideal collaboration formula, featuring questions that everyone should ask themselves before pressing “go” on a partnership that could fall flat.

* Vocelli Pizza [names](https://substack.com/redirect/c5cfc94d-bb4b-4e07-936d-94ee5bf5f6ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Love Advertising Marketing AOR.
* Publicis [moves](https://substack.com/redirect/e33f85d1-b8c5-43e1-aa67-32de129d1122?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Lloyds account into bespoke team.
* [Havas Media Network](https://substack.com/redirect/b865a9c7-7e3c-49b9-a72d-2cf002c28469?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) named AOR by Early Warning Services.
* [HomeEquity Bank](https://substack.com/redirect/c5e98888-b3b3-4a1a-86d7-80fbaf3f0e45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) names Lifelong Crush AOR.
	+ Hockey Canada [selects](https://substack.com/redirect/aacb6752-e01a-42e1-8441-1656d1c409e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Diamond as Digital Agency of Record.
* Broken Heart Love Affair (BHLA) [named](https://substack.com/redirect/28ab65a4-a951-4b07-b9f9-d5513a73f46a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Agency of Record for United Way Greater Toronto (UWGT).
* Visit Victoria [pitches](https://substack.com/redirect/33aa9b59-25d2-4d1f-ab59-6580ab328d0a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creative account.
* Gérard Bertrand taps [MMGY Wagstaff](https://substack.com/redirect/a24f1fec-ac84-4e55-8cd1-bc57bef6e637?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for US PR and marketing push.
* KFC Singapore appoints [Maker Lab](https://substack.com/redirect/372ca188-c9b3-4beb-a3a4-0c4247d27e60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Creative and Social Agency of Record.
* [Dentsu Creative](https://substack.com/redirect/387a7d34-e9aa-4343-be16-0458201b845f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) hits jackpot in Sky Gaming creative review.
* 7-Eleven Canada appoints [Publicis Toronto](https://substack.com/redirect/8ef4a032-bf66-48cd-b309-bd99f4d0a783?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Creative Agency of Record.
* Connecting Plots wins [Placemaking NSW’s $3.6M](https://substack.com/redirect/85c8be67-d8ba-4abf-8bf9-4e0537f206ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creative & strategic account.
* E.ON Next appoints [TSA](https://substack.com/redirect/b6016be1-9286-455a-84b3-b345059c2f77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as new social creative agency.
* [Merlin Entertainments](https://substack.com/redirect/095f3f52-6694-4d13-8030-e356d32b27df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) appoints Omnicom agencies OMD and Adam & Eve/DDB as its global media and creative agencies.
* OPPO appoints [Stagwell agencies](https://substack.com/redirect/b7035fc8-ddfb-4db6-8451-14bf3bf7ef9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Singapore.
* [Serviceplan Middle East](https://substack.com/redirect/1e2c2843-9dd2-423d-8c88-241671656689?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) retains global creative mandate for DCT Abu Dhabi.
* Zinpro selects [broadhead](https://substack.com/redirect/ebfcb818-6a0b-48b9-8b3e-dd825ac2e661?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Agency of Record for Multiple Divisions.

---

Back on Sunday. Until then…

The Social Juice - Best of Marketing on Instagram: "Art Nouveau…

***The Social Juice+** includes access to newsletter archive and strategy newsletters. **It’s your decision, But you are losing all three benefits**. When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/8df6c687-3c19-46b6-a655-180fec37576d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

*\* **Others:** [Student Discount](https://substack.com/redirect/2e62a4ee-51a3-4541-bd4f-d0fcf082fb4b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & Localised pricing for [Asia](https://substack.com/redirect/dacbf464-79b0-456a-b2c9-39179ccffa72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [Eastern Europe](https://substack.com/redirect/5b99547a-fc28-4b9f-967e-ec772e4d57d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), [South America](https://substack.com/redirect/14ea9357-1b78-41dc-873d-c5bea615c246?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) & [Africa](https://substack.com/redirect/9e2a5c82-9668-423d-b244-1d7eda195abf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).*

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. </span><em>Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</em></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Brand &amp; Advertising coverage from 05.01 — 17.01.2026….</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8971fc74-a368-417b-85db-8d06e55a1cdd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d8b7db11-42e9-439a-8a99-52dc8afb8a96?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":67342,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!JnHP!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F01e1bfca-7b3a-4e58-80e6-f8bb4cb10eb2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>SXSW Sydney </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb02e2e7-b576-4497-8931-19ee82d412d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">scrapped</a><span> after three years </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cbaf663f-250a-44f5-9542-fb9763deb90f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">due to</a><span> ‘changing global environment’, organisers announce.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Humble </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a099acf0-c48d-4558-bacb-aeb0a54d3545?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hershey’s</a><span> gets a marketing makeover.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Influencers and OnlyFans models are increasingly</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bda9fb43-83a6-4f12-bd08-2362d710cd97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> requesting O-1 visas</a><span>: ‘This is the American dream now.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Advertising group Dentsu’s push to sell global unit close to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57df8cc1-c905-4b98-87e7-d1875b378491?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">collapse</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Walt Disney Company names </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f72c87cb-0702-4ac0-badd-d9d4fd826ad4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Asad Ayaz</a><span> as first-ever Chief Marketing Officer.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Luxury department store chain Saks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/db97f347-da6b-47f3-8e1c-98e65601a9bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">files for bankruptcy</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aa5dd4aa-537f-4787-8986-c10281e64027?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">threatens</a><span> ‘drastic’ action after Saks bankruptcy, says $475M stake is now worthless.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5543e4bd-e9d1-4337-a187-d1bea64bed9d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">2016</a><span> Was Actually </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/48d25cf0-1117-401c-bfcd-35ab17017ee3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Worst Year</a><span> Ever, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13f9d627-998c-44e8-8b07-0328ba91a96f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Remember</a><span>?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>L Catterton strikes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b43a36d-8a1c-499e-8f10-dc1ed8bfdfe4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$500 million-plus deal</a><span> for cottage cheese brand Good Culture.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea184d98-f390-4b55-8ffb-f24971cd59af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> reportedly readies </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1625ec7a-94d4-4b5c-b8ac-bad591683d38?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lengthy Super Bowl</a><span> commercial.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Tracksuit’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">Collab of the Year Report</a><span> confirms that effective collaborations aren’t about reach, but about resonance.</span>
</h3>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1699"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aef9a460-ccc1-4bf2-baea-5c9cdb9aa049?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/5df9586f-b9d8-4e69-a660-6453f56f1b5a\_1699x624.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":624,"width":1699,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":446401,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/184748655?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0e1d9239-c3e9-4739-bcd6-c811437b5f82\_1699x741.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="202.00117716303708" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!5vyc!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5df9586f-b9d8-4e69-a660-6453f56f1b5a\_1699x624.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The biggest lesson from the year’s top sneaker collaborations is that </span><em><strong>scale is secondary, and specificity is everything.</strong></em><span> The partnerships that created the most impact weren’t the loudest, the most famous, or most widely seen. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Their data</a><span> shows that the collaborations that moved brands forward weren’t designed for everyone, they were designed for someone. That is, they were built for a small, passionate audience, which proved to be more valuable than millions of passive viewers. </span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>For example, Tracksuit and Bimma’s top collaboration (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">download the report</a><span> to find out which one this is!) achieved only 4% general awareness, but 67% among fans familiar with the brand and collaborator. Alignment is the engine.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>“The strongest collaborations don’t chase virality. They build meaning,” said Matt Herbert, Co-founder of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f030d908-875c-4edd-8a22-6c5f04864f3f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>. “When brands design for the right audience with the right partner, they create depth instead of noise.”</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><span>The 2025 Collab of the Year report is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a new playbook</a><span> for marketers: </span></strong></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>You can use the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/57afc229-3c16-4445-bb67-3e3c32c9d21b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span> to help guide your own brand collaborations, regardless of category. This is a framework that breaks down the ideal collaboration formula, featuring questions that everyone should ask themselves before pressing “go” on a partnership that could fall flat.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/948c76f0-4aef-4a02-a5f9-2624dfc83535?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Shirley Temple</a><span> is back for Dry January, plus </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/619cb638-d627-489d-896f-1cbc9936900b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more nostalgic beverages</a><span> bubbling up.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Laptop Boyfriends Can’t Stop Watching </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3aefaec7-5e66-4444-a7cd-e705b5853fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">YouTube</a><span> in Bed.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d265a939-1ad6-4422-8370-e4306f245a9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Looking for a pen?</a><span> Shoppers overwhelm Chicago stationery shops as social media touts ‘return to analog.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09800bf2-b75a-4401-827c-b789285a7286?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IMAX CEO</a><span>: Much more demand from filmmakers for our tech.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Young Americans are</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0f2a2275-3f3a-4dca-aa92-16090a81bde9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> increasingly rejecting</a><span> the Democratic and Republican parties, a new poll shows.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China’s retail sector heads into the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3006a28-66fd-47d7-a437-f48b65231d35?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">era of me</a><span>’, says NielsenIQ.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>L’Oréal opens </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eb9c0def-ea38-440c-af0f-850f6789d1dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New York mega-hub for content creation</a><span>, stylist education and more.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brand Safety on Steroids: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c36fe807-7120-4af4-bc01-7bc8f2e22e9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hermés</a><span> is stalking their </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21b4c987-ad3e-4c54-9d24-b07c0c322f18?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">clients</a><span>?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7345c9af-c417-413c-ae77-8987b79c0201?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pitti Uomo 109</a><span>: the street style </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/27d774dd-64ed-4119-b57a-5bfbe472961c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">setting the tone</a><span> for 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>NY Mag is bringing back “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cdb1acda-919b-40df-a097-67a1a4ec69bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Classifieds</a><span>” for New York-based subscribers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/63489103-faf0-49fb-aec5-2278cb995b1d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hudson Williams</a><span> just made his Milan Fashion Week debut.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AIGA NY’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21c0e4a9-7ff2-4cf2-9daa-2dea9c68112e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">poster archive charts</a><span> five decades of civic discourse and design.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="1100"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3a8f0e9c-6688-4b84-9c05-ee21293364c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":65322,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/164226189?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!\_L36!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F22cabaf0-a437-483c-a9fb-acf71ce426f2\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Dunkin' is celebrating its iconic collaboration with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/97cb0c1a-b807-46ce-8ab5-f69e6530f84e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">artist Megan Thee Stallion</a><span> by giving out free </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6a6679d1-6b38-4b38-989e-0d11ebf59bd3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">limited-edition</a><span> reusable cups.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1fad374-4edc-4150-86f4-f04357973143?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">2016</a><span>: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0e1c641e-0de9-489e-8f43-d266823081fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Brands</a><span> are posting </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ee838e40-27ea-42f5-b633-44f48a468d53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">throwbacks</a><span>, including </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1e0c7a5d-ca82-4ca6-a40f-25bc41369498?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/027eee4f-af83-4978-bc54-57053db4bd26?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">King Kyle</a><span>. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Julia Fox talks exclusively to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ae4c1230-cd4e-4c9b-b540-76195e08efee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">NYLON</a><span> about her </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/925d7b7f-7862-4362-988c-b869a3cecff0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">David Protein Bronze Bar</a><span> campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e7dc2e76-8f18-46b7-9138-7acec1f13c31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">M&amp;M’S</a><span> suits up as heroes in global Marvel campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8f225e64-3a1e-42bd-bb64-d1b6451b0057?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bad Bunny</a><span> and Apple Music invite the fans and strangers to dance at the Super Bowl, the vibes are immaculate. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nintendo has been forced to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cf287b77-2d4c-4896-bf0b-f6393ddaf3c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">deny reports it used AI-generated imagery</a><span> to advertise its new My Mario children’s toy range.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Jeep ad campaign pokes fun at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/770845dc-dcde-4bff-b7b2-55647af445fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">faux off-roaders</a><span>.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21bad81c-7e64-4b56-8de0-d890ef16b127?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Harry Potter”</a><span> installation in Berlin lights up in celebration of new TV series.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>LEGO Education launches a new campaign, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/461aaca0-44ab-46ab-83de-db11e8c92e7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">We Trust in Kids</a><span>, that flips the AI education conversation by handing it directly to students.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>BuildHollywood x Uncle </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/853d31c8-c1ae-4da7-8d5b-2b36f42108a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">celebrate love</a><span> with nationwide street gallery.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Havas launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a55cda4b-3536-4dc5-bf52-91b08df8f899?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Global LLM Port, AVA</a><span> with GPT-5 and Gemini 3 AI models.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/64e3b223-dea3-44ee-b469-003034b756f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Omnicom</a><span> unveils its much-awaited AI-driven platform ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8170387d-4f6d-4fed-8e48-2af0ce5cb74e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New Omni</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WPP Open unveils </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8845964c-2166-4cba-99c7-093e29907cee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Agent Hub </a><span>to make ‘agency expertise available to entire business’.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Stagwell unveils Agentic OS ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a1550e77-a2d0-41b8-b99b-06aef2d9c3e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Machine</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>PMG announces the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/32ae386c-1cf1-4474-91db-45276e5733bc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> acquisition of London and New York-based Digital Voices</a><span>, a leading influencer marketing agency.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>FDA </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/521b0545-fdb6-484f-8a8c-d67564eb83ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">continues</a><span> ad enforcement push with first two untitled letters 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2fba9c35-0d61-4ed9-a6e4-a9a716957921?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Global</a><span> buys Gary Neville’s The Overlap media venture.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Media buyers say </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/211d31d4-f84e-47b0-bb45-d0e724bdba93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Yahoo’s DSP</a><span> is one to watch.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Stagwell expands </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/689c8104-a52b-483b-b0c1-c46d148b4c51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sport Beach</a><span>, taps Sidhu to run it as CEO.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Stagwell </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e843f59b-caed-4719-8315-4eaeab89cc77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unifies</a><span> media, commerce ops under Samardzija.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Europe-wide cross-media measurement study </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8fb0fe40-32a5-4b95-b464-e7b569abcc21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to begin</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
